what are the top 10 side effects reported for drug tromethamine?
PRED: select t1.icd9_code, d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from ( select diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from diagnoses_icd join prescriptions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'tromethamine' group by diagnoses_icd.icd9_code ) as t1 join d_icd_diagnoses on t1.icd9_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code where t1.c1 <= 10
GOLD: null

what are the top 10 side effects reported for drug tromethamine?
----------
PREDS:
select t1.icd9_code, d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from ( select diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from diagnoses_icd join prescriptions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'tromethamine' group by diagnoses_icd.icd9_code ) as t1 join d_icd_diagnoses on t1.icd9_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code where t1.c1 <= 10
----------
TRUTH:
null

what doctor did patient 18866 see at last in the general surgery department?
PRED: select distinct d_items.label from d_items where d_items.itemid in (
  select inputevents_cv.itemid from inputevents_cv 
  where inputevents_cv.hadm_id in (
    select hadm_id from admissions where subject_id = 18866
  ) and inputevents_cv.charttime = (
    select max(inputevents_cv.charttime) from inputevents_cv 
    join admissions on inputevents_cv.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    where admissions.subject_id = 18866 and inputevents_cv.charttime is not null
  )
) and d_items.label like '%general surgery%' limit 1;
GOLD: null

what doctor did patient 18866 see at last in the general surgery department?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct d_items.label from d_items where d_items.itemid in (
  select inputevents_cv.itemid from inputevents_cv 
  where inputevents_cv.hadm_id in (
    select hadm_id from admissions where subject_id = 18866
  ) and inputevents_cv.charttime = (
    select max(inputevents_cv.charttime) from inputevents_cv 
    join admissions on inputevents_cv.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    where admissions.subject_id = 18866 and inputevents_cv.charttime is not null
  )

what are the 10 most common side effects of clarithromycin??
PRED: select t1.LONG_TITLE as side_effect from ( select diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, count(*) as cnt from diagnoses_icd join prescriptions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'clarithromycin' group by diagnoses_icd.icd9_code order by cnt desc limit 10 ) as t join d_icd_diagnoses t1 on t.icd9_code = t1.icd9_code;
GOLD: null

what is the top 10 side effect of magnesium sulfate (ob)?
PRED: select t3.icd9_code, d.short_title from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id join prescriptions on admissions.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'magnesium sulfate' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 join d_icd_diagnoses d on t3.icd9_code = d.icd9_code where t3.c1 <= 10 order by t3.c1 asc;
GOLD: null

what is the top 10 side effect of magnesium sulfate (ob)?
----------
PREDS:
select t3.icd9_code, d.short_title from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id join prescriptions on admissions.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'magnesium sulfate' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 join d_icd_diagnoses d on t3.icd9_code = d.icd9_code where t3.c1 <= 10 order by t3.c1 asc;

what is the top 10 side effect of lovastatin?
PRED: select t3.icd9_code, d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'lovastatin' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where datetime(t2.charttime) > datetime(t1.startdate) and datetime(t2.charttime) <= datetime(t1.startdate,'+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 join d_icd_diagnoses on t3.icd9_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code where t3.c1 <= 10 order by t3.c1 asc
GOLD: null

what is the top 10 side effect of lovastatin?

select t3.icd9_code, d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'lovastatin' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where datetime(t2.charttime) > datetime(t1.startdate) and datetime(t2.charttime) <= datetime(t1.startdate,'+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 join d_icd_diagnoses on t3.icd9_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code where t3.c1 <= 10 order by t3.c1 asc

what are the most common side effects attributed to cimetidine?
PRED: select d.short_title from (
  select di.icd9_code, dense_rank() over (order by count(*) desc) as rnk
  from diagnoses_icd di
  join prescriptions p on di.hadm_id = p.hadm_id
  where p.drug = 'cimetidine'
  group by di.icd9_code
) as top_codes
join d_icd_diagnoses d on top_codes.icd9_code = d.icd9_code
where top_codes.rnk <= 5;
GOLD: null

what are the most common side effects attributed to cimetidine?
----------
PREDS:
select d.short_title from (
  select di.icd9_code, dense_rank() over (order by count(*) desc) as rnk
  from diagnoses_icd di
  join prescriptions p on di.hadm_id = p.hadm_id
  where p.drug = 'cimetidine'
  group by di.icd9_code
) as top_codes
join d_icd_diagnoses d on top_codes.icd9_code = d.icd9_code
where top_codes.rnk <= 5;
----------
TRUTH:
null
----------

PRED: select t3.icd9_code, d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id join diagnoses_icd on admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'propranolol hcl' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from admissions join diagnoses_icd on admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where datetime(t2.charttime) > datetime(t1.startdate) and datetime(t2.charttime) <= datetime(t1.startdate, '+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 join d_icd_diagnoses on t3.icd9_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code where t3.c1 <= 10 order by t3.c1 asc;
GOLD: null

what is the top 10 side effect of propranolol hcl?
----------
PREDS:
select t3.icd9_code, d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id join diagnoses_icd on admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'propranolol hcl' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from admissions join diagnoses_icd on admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where datetime(t2.charttime) > datetime(t1.startdate) and datetime(t2.charttime) <= datetime(t1.startdate, '+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 join d_icd_diagnoses on t3.icd9_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code where t3.c1 <= 10 order by t3.c1 asc;
----------
TRUTH:
null
----------

        where subject_id = 54894
    )
    and diagnoses_icd.hadm_id not in (
        select hadm_id 
        from admissions 
        where subject_id = 54894 
        and admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id
    )
    limit 1
)

what's the average wait time for appointments in physiotherapy?
PRED: select avg(julianday(charttime) - julianday(admittime)) * 24 * 60 as avg_wait_minutes
from chartevents
join d_items on chartevents.itemid = d_items.itemid
join admissions on chartevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
where d_items.label = 'physical therapy' and charttime > admittime;
GOLD: null

what is the average period of delay between appointments in the nephrology department?
PRED: SELECT AVG(julianday(next_transfer.INTIME) - julianday(curr_transfer.OUTTIME)) AS avg_delay_days
FROM TRANSFERS curr_transfer
JOIN TRANSFERS next_transfer ON curr_transfer.SUBJECT_ID = next_transfer.SUBJECT_ID
  AND curr_transfer.HADM_ID = next_transfer.HADM_ID
  AND next_transfer.INTIME > curr_transfer.OUTTIME
  AND next_transfer.EVENTTYPE = 'admit'
WHERE curr_transfer.CAREUNIT = 'nephrology'
  AND curr_transfer.EVENTTYPE = 'discharge';
GOLD: null

what is the average period of delay between appointments in the nephrology department?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(julianday(next_transfer.INTIME) - julianday(curr_transfer.OUTTIME)) AS avg_delay_days
FROM TRANSFERS curr_transfer
JOIN TRANSFERS next_transfer ON curr_transfer.SUBJECT_ID = next_transfer.SUBJECT_ID
  AND curr_transfer.HADM_ID = next_transfer.HADM_ID
  AND next_transfer.INTIME > curr_transfer.OUTTIME
  AND next_transfer.EVENTTYPE = 'admit'
WHERE curr_transfer.CAREUNIT = 'nephrology'
  AND curr_transfer.EVENTTYPE = 'discharge';
----------
TRUTH:
null
----------

what is the average time between appointments in the orthopedics department?
PRED: select avg(julianday(next_admittime) - julianday(admittime)) * 24 * 60 as avg_minutes_between_appointments
from (
  select admittime, 
         lead(admittime) over (order by admittime) as next_admittime
  from admissions
  where admission_location = 'orthopedics'
) where next_admittime is not null;
GOLD: null

what is the average time between appointments in the orthopedics department?
----------
PREDS:
select avg(julianday(next_admittime) - julianday(admittime)) * 24 * 60 as avg_minutes_between_appointments
from (
  select admittime, 
         lead(admittime) over (order by admittime) as next_admittime
  from admissions
  where admission_location = 'orthopedics'
) where next_admittime is not null;
----------
TRUTH:
null

    FROM appointments
    WHERE department = 'radiology'

what is the average period of delay between appointments in the rheumatology department?
PRED: select avg(julianday(next_transfer.intime) - julianday(curr_transfer.outtime)) as avg_delay_days
from transfers curr_transfer
join transfers next_transfer on curr_transfer.subject_id = next_transfer.subject_id and curr_transfer.hadm_id = next_transfer.hadm_id and next_transfer.intime > curr_transfer.outtime
where curr_transfer.careunit = 'rheumatology' and next_transfer.eventtype = 'transfer' and curr_transfer.eventtype = 'transfer' and next_transfer.intime = (
    select min(t2.intime) from transfers t2 where t2.subject_id = curr_transfer.subject_id and t2.hadm_id = curr_transfer.hadm_id and t2.intime > curr_transfer.outtime
)
GOLD: null

what is the average period of delay between appointments in the rheumatology department?
----------
PREDS:
select avg(julianday(next_transfer.intime) - julianday(curr_transfer.outtime)) as avg_delay_days
from transfers curr_transfer
join transfers next_transfer on curr_transfer.subject_id = next_transfer.subject_id and curr_transfer.hadm_id = next_transfer.hadm_id and next_transfer.intime > curr_transfer.outtime
where curr_transfer.careunit = 'rheumatology' and next_transfer.eventtype = 'transfer' and curr_transfer.eventtype = 'transfer' and next_transfer.intime = (
    select min(t2.intime) from transfers t2 where t2.subject_id = curr_transfer.subject_id and t2.hadm_id = curr_transfer.hadm_id and t2.intime > curr_transfer.outtime
)
----------
TRUTH:
null
----------

what is the average time of delay for appointments in the gastroenterology department?
PRED: SELECT AVG(appointment_delay) AS average_delay
FROM (
  SELECT 
    julianday(scheduled_time) * 24 * 60 + julianday(scheduled_time) * 24 * 60 - julianday(actual_time) * 24 * 60 AS appointment_delay
  FROM appointments
  WHERE department = 'gastroenterology'
) subquery;
GOLD: null

what is the average time of delay for appointments in the gastroenterology department?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(appointment_delay) AS average_delay
FROM (
  SELECT 
    julianday(scheduled_time) * 24 * 60 + julianday(scheduled_time) * 24 * 60 - julianday(actual_time) * 24 * 60 AS appointment_delay
  FROM appointments
  WHERE department = 'gastroenterology'
) subquery;
----------
TRUTH:
null

what is the drug to avoid during the exc/dest hrt lesion open.
PRED: select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'exc/dest hrt lesion open' ) ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 = 1
GOLD: null

what is the drug to avoid during the exc/dest hrt lesion open.
----------
PREDS:
select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'exc/dest hrt lesion open' ) ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 = 1
----------
TRUTH:
null

retrieve patient 5865's daily average temperature c (calc) during their first hospital encounter.
----------
PREDS:
select date(charttime) as day, avg(valuenum) as daily_avg_temperature_c_calc
from chartevents
where subject_id = 5865
and icustay_id in (
  select icustay_id from icustays
  where hadm_id in (
    select hadm_id from admissions
    where subject_id = 5865
  )
  order by intime asc
  limit 1
)
and itemid in (
  select itemid from d_items where label = 'temperature c (calc)'
)
group by day
order by day asc;
{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['chartevents'], 'cond': [['label', '=', 'temperature c (calc) ) group by day order by day asc;']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select avg(chartevents.valuenum) from chartevents where chartevents.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 5865 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'temperature c (calc)' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) group by strftime('%y-%m-%d',chartevents.charttime)
{'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['chartevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['chartevents'], 'cond': [['d_items.label', '=', 'temperature c (calc)'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents ) group by strftime( %y-%m-%d ,chartevents.charttime)']]}

tell me the average total hospital cost that includes endosc remove bile stone in this year?
----------
PREDS:
select avg(t1.c1) from ( select sum(cost.cost) as c1 from cost where cost.hadm_id in ( select procedures_icd.hadm_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'endosc remove bile stone' ) ) and strftime('%Y',cost.chargetime) = strftime('%Y','now') group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1
{'sel': 'avg', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'endosc remove bile stone ) )'], ["strftime('%y',cost.chargetime)", '=', 'strftime( %y , now ) group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select avg(t1.c1) from ( select sum(cost.cost) as c1 from cost where cost.hadm_id in ( select procedures_icd.hadm_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'endosc remove bile stone' ) ) and datetime(cost.chargetime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1

tell me the average cost of a hospital if wbc, csf is involved since 3 years ago?
----------
PREDS:
select avg(t1.c1) from (
  select sum(cost.cost) as c1 from cost 
  where cost.hadm_id in (
    select labevents.hadm_id from labevents 
    where labevents.itemid in (
      select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'wbc'
    )
    and labevents.hadm_id in (
      select labevents.hadm_id from labevents 
      where labevents.itemid in (
        select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'csf'
      )
    )
  )
  and datetime(cost.chargetime) >= datetime(current_time,'-3 year')
  group by cost.hadm_id
) as t1
{'sel': 'avg', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'csf ) ) )'], ['datetime(cost.chargetime)', '>=', 'datetime(current_time, -3 year ) group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select avg(t1.c1) from ( select sum(cost.cost) as c1 from cost where cost.hadm_id in ( select labevents.hadm_id from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'wbc, csf' ) ) and datetime(cost.chargetime) >= datetime(current_time,'-3 year') group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1
{'sel': 'avg', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'wbc, csf ) )'], ['datetime(cost.chargetime)', '>=', 'datetime(current_time, -3 year ) group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1']]}

tell me the maximum total hospital cost that includes albumin, pleural until 2 years ago?
----------
PREDS:
select max(t1.c1) from (
  select sum(cost.cost) as c1 
  from cost 
  where cost.hadm_id in (
    select labevents.hadm_id 
    from labevents 
    where labevents.itemid in (
      select d_labitems.itemid 
      from d_labitems 
      where d_labitems.label = 'albumin'
    )
  )
  and cost.hadm_id in (
    select chartevents.hadm_id 
    from chartevents 
    where chartevents.itemid in (
      select d_items.itemid 
      from d_items 
      where d_items.label = 'pleural'
    )
  )
  and cost.chargetime <= datetime('now','-2 years')
  group by cost.hadm_id
) as t1
{'sel': 'max', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['cost.chargetime', '<=', 'datetime( now , -2 years ) group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'pleural ) )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select max(t1.c1) from ( select sum(cost.cost) as c1 from cost where cost.hadm_id in ( select labevents.hadm_id from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'albumin, pleural' ) ) and datetime(cost.chargetime) <= datetime(current_time,'-2 year') group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1
{'sel': 'max', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'albumin, pleural ) )'], ['datetime(cost.chargetime)', '<=', 'datetime(current_time, -2 year ) group by cost.hadm_id ) as t1']]}

count the number of patients that died within the same hospital visit after having been diagnosed with septicemia nos.
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t2.subject_id) 
from (
    select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime, t1.hadm_id 
    from (
        select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        from diagnoses_icd 
        join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (
            select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code 
            from d_icd_diagnoses 
            where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'septicemia nos'
        )
    ) as t1 
    group by t1.subject_id, t1.hadm_id, t1.charttime 
    having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime
) as t2 
join (
    select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod 
    from admissions 
    join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id 
    where patients.dod is not null
) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id and t2.hadm_id = t3.hadm_id;
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id'], ['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id;']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select count( distinct t2.subject_id ) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime, t1.hadm_id from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'septicemia nos' ) ) as t1 group by t1.subject_id having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime ) as t2 join ( select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id where patients.dod is not null ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where t2.hadm_id = t3.hadm_id
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id']]}

count the number of patients that died within 2 months after having been diagnosed with ulcer other part of foot this year.
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t2.subject_id) from (
  select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from (
    select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd 
    join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (
      select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'ulcer other part of foot'
    )
  ) as t1 
  group by t1.subject_id 
  having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime and strftime('%Y', t1.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
) as t2 
join (
  select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions 
  join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id 
  where patients.dod is not null and strftime('%Y', patients.dod) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id 
where datetime(t3.dod) between datetime(t2.charttime) and datetime(t2.charttime, '+2 month') or datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod) and datetime(t3.dod, '+2 month')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['datetime(t2.charttime,', "'+2", 'month ) or datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod)'], ['datetime(t3.dod)', 'between', 'datetime(t2.charttime)'], ['datetime(t3.dod,', "'+2", 'month )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select count( distinct t2.subject_id ) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'ulcer other part of foot' ) ) as t1 group by t1.subject_id having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as t2 join ( select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id where patients.dod is not null and datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where ( datetime(t3.dod) between datetime(t2.charttime) and datetime(t2.charttime,'+2 month') ) or ( datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod) and datetime(t3.dod,'+2 month') )
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['(', 'datetime(t3.dod)', 'between datetime(t2.charttime)'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'+2", "month')", ') or ( datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod)'], ["datetime(t3.dod,'+2", "month')", ')']]}

   where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'esophageal reflux')) as t1 
join 
  (select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
   from diagnoses_icd 
   join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'single lb in-hosp w cs')) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
where t1.charttime > t2.charttime and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '>', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

TRUTH:

how many patients were diagnosed with obs chr bronc w(ac) exac until 4 years ago after they were diagnosed with aortocoronary bypass within 2 months?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) 
from (
    select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    from diagnoses_icd 
    join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (
        select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code 
        from d_icd_diagnoses 
        where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'obs chr bronc w(ac) exac'
    ) 
    and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) <= datetime(current_time, '-4 years')
) as t1 
join (
    select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    from procedures_icd 
    join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (
        select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code 
        from d_icd_procedures 
        where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'aortocoronary bypass nos'
    ) 
    and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) <= datetime(current_time, '-4 years')
) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
where t1.charttime < t2.charttime 
and datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime, '+2 month')

how many patients were diagnosed with hypothyroidism nos in the same hospital visit after the diagnosis with both eyes blind-who def, since 2105?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) 
from 
  (select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
   from diagnoses_icd 
   join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'hypothyroidism nos') 
     and diagnoses_icd.charttime >= '2105-01-01') as t1 
join 
  (select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
   from diagnoses_icd 
   join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'both eyes blind-who def') 
     and diagnoses_icd.charttime >= '2105-01-01') as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id 
where t1.charttime > t2.charttime
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '>', 't2.charttime']]}

during a year before, how many patients were diagnosed with long-term use of insulin in the same month after the diagnosis of pulm congest/hypostasis?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) 
from (
  select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
  from diagnoses_icd 
  join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_diagnoses 
    where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'long-term use of insulin'
  ) 
  and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') >= datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-1 year')
) as t1 
join (
  select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
  from diagnoses_icd 
  join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_diagnoses 
    where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'pulm congest/hypostasis'
  ) 
  and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') >= datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-1 year')
) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
where t1.charttime > t2.charttime 
and datetime(t1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['datetime(t1.charttime,', "'start", 'of month ) = datetime(t2.charttime, start of month )'], ['t1.charttime', '>', 't2.charttime']]}

how many patients received a pericardiocentesis procedure within the same month after receiving a open liver biopsy procedure during this year?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) 
from 
  (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
   from procedures_icd 
   join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'open liver biopsy') 
     and strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')) as t1 
join 
  (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
   from procedures_icd 
   join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'pericardiocentesis') 
     and strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
where t1.charttime < t2.charttime 
  and datetime(t1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['datetime(t1.charttime,', "'start", 'of month ) = datetime(t2.charttime, start of month )'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'open liver biopsy' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'pericardiocentesis' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [["datetime(t1.charttime,'start", 'of', 'month ) = datetime(t2.charttime, start of month )'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------

how many patients have had lower limb artery incis during the same hospital visit after the cont inv mec ven <96 hrs since 3 years ago?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from 
  (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
   from procedures_icd 
   join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'cont inv mec ven <96 hrs') 
     and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-3 years')) as t1

  (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
   from procedures_icd 
   join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
   where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'lower limb artery incis') 
     and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-3 years')) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
where t1.charttime < t2.charttime 
  and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id;
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id;']]}

TRUTH:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

how many patients have had coronar arteriogr-2 cath within 2 months after the extracorporeal circulat during a year before?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from (
  select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
  from procedures_icd 
  join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_procedures 
    where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'extracorporeal circulat'
  ) 
  and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
) as t1 
join (
  select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
  from procedures_icd 
  join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_procedures 
    where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'coronar arteriogr-2 cath'
  ) 
  and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
where t1.charttime < t2.charttime 
and datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime, '+2 month')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['datetime(t1.charttime,', "'+2", 'month )'], ['datetime(t2.charttime)', 'between', 'datetime(t1.charttime)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['datetime(t2.charttime)', 'between', 'datetime(t1.charttime)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from (
  select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
  from diagnoses_icd 
  join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_diagnoses 
    where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'pleural effusion nos'
  ) 
  and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
) as t1 
join (
  select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
  from procedures_icd 
  join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_procedures 
    where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'vaccination nec'
  ) 
  and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id 
where t1.charttime < t2.charttime
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

count the number of patients who had been prescribed phenylephrine hcl during the same hospital visit after a inser temp pacemaker sys procedure.
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from (
  select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
  from procedures_icd 
  join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (
    select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code 
    from d_icd_procedures 
    where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'inser temp pacemaker sys'
  )
) as t1 
join (
  select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate, admissions.hadm_id 
  from prescriptions 
  join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  where prescriptions.drug = 'phenylephrine hcl'
) as t2 
on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id 
and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id 
where t1.charttime < t2.startdate
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.startdate']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'inser temp pacemaker sys' ) ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate, admissions.hadm_id from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'phenylephrine hcl' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.startdate'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

  ) 
  and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year')

among patients who were diagnosed with subendo infarct, initial since 2105, what are the top five most commonly prescribed drugs that followed afterwards during the same hospital visit to the male patients aged 20s?
----------
PREDS:

{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'subendo infarct, initial' ) and strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2105' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate, admissions.hadm_id from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where admissions.subject_id in ( select patients.subject_id from patients where patients.gender = 'm' ) and admissions.age between 20 and 29 and strftime('%y',prescriptions.startdate) >= '2105' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 5
{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

among patients who received fiber-optic bronchoscopy in 2104, what are the top three most frequent cultures tested afterwards in the same month?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct MICROBIOLOGYEVENTS.SPEC_TYPE_DESC from MICROBIOLOGYEVENTS where MICROBIOLOGYEVENTS.HADM_ID in ( select procedures_icd.hadm_id from procedures_icd join d_icd_procedures on procedures_icd.icd9_code = d_icd_procedures.icd9_code where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'fiber-optic bronchoscopy' and strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2104' ) and MICROBIOLOGYEVENTS.charttime > ( select min(procedures_icd.charttime) from procedures_icd join d_icd_procedures on procedures_icd.icd9_code = d_icd_procedures.icd9_code where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'fiber-optic bronchoscopy' and strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2104' ) and strftime('%Y-%m',MICROBIOLOGYEVENTS.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',( select min(procedures_icd.charttime) from procedures_icd join d_icd_procedures on procedures_icd.icd9_code = d_icd_procedures.icd9_code where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'fiber-optic bronchoscopy' and strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2104' )) group by MICROBIOLOGYEVENTS.SPEC_TYPE_DESC order by count(*) desc limit 3;
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'fiber-optic bronchoscopy'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2104 )) group by microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc order by count(*) desc limit 3;']]}
